### test_content_con007_con012_remediations.cpp

#### `TEST(AsyncIngestionWorkerCON012, DestructorIsNoexcept)`
- Source: `tests/content/test_content_con007_con012_remediations.cpp`:110
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (AsyncIngestionWorkerCON012): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (DestructorIsNoexcept): n/a
- Details: TestCON-012 compile-time check: AsyncIngestionWorker::~AsyncIngestionWorker() must be noexcept. std::is_nothrow_destructible_v is true when the destructor is declared noexcept (or is implicitly noexcept). This check does not require constructing a live object, so no RocksDB or ContentManager setup is needed. Pre-fix: the destructor was declared without noexcept, so this assertion would have failed.

#### `TEST(VideoProcessorHealthCheckCON007, AfterShutdown_AlwaysUnhealthy)`

- Brief: n/a

  - `<unnamed>` (AfterShutdown_AlwaysUnhealthy): n/a
- Details: TestCON-007: shutdown resets health regardless of build.

- Brief: n/a

  - `<unnamed>` (VideoProcessorHealthCheckCON007): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (FFmpegMode_InitializedAndHealthy): n/a
- Details: TestCON-007 regression guard: in an FFmpeg build an initialized VideoProcessor must still report healthy (the FFmpeg path is unchanged).

#### `TEST(VideoProcessorHealthCheckCON007, NotInitialized_AlwaysUnhealthy)`
- Source: `tests/content/test_content_con007_con012_remediations.cpp`:38
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (VideoProcessorHealthCheckCON007): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (NotInitialized_AlwaysUnhealthy): n/a
- Details: TestAn un-initialized VideoProcessor must always report unhealthy, regardless of FFmpeg availability. This is a baseline invariant.

#### `TEST(VideoProcessorHealthCheckCON007, SimulationMode_InitializedButUnhealthy)`
- Source: `tests/content/test_content_con007_con012_remediations.cpp`:52
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (VideoProcessorHealthCheckCON007): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (SimulationMode_InitializedButUnhealthy): n/a
- Details: TestCON-007 regression: in a no-FFmpeg (simulation-mode) build an initialized VideoProcessor must report unhealthy so aggregators can surface the missing FFmpeg dependency. Pre-fix behaviour: both FFmpeg and no-FFmpeg branches returned initialized_, which is true after initialize() — the no-FFmpeg path was silent.

#### `TEST(DedupOverheadPerf, PerceptualDedupAddsLessThan10PctOverhead)`
- Source: `tests/content/test_content_deduplication.cpp`:798
- Brief: Near-duplicate detection overhead: < 10% of total ingestion proxy latency.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (DedupOverheadPerf): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (PerceptualDedupAddsLessThan10PctOverhead): n/a
- Details: Measures the incremental cost of perceptual deduplication (pHash or MinHash + band-LSH lookup) relative to a baseline that only does exact-dedup (SHA-256). Uses the text path (MinHash) with a warm 100 K-entry band index to exercise the realistic worst-case lookup path. Performance target from acceptance criteria (roadmap:168:content:v1.8.0, AC-8). Set THEMIS_RUN_PERF_TESTS=1 to enable.

#### `TEST_F(ContentSHA256DedupTest, PerceptualDedupDefaultsToOffWhenKeyAbsent)`
- Source: `tests/content/test_content_deduplication.cpp`:592
- Brief: Verify that when enable_deduplication is absent from config (default), perceptual dedup is NOT performed even when a DeduplicationChecker is attached.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (ContentSHA256DedupTest): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (PerceptualDedupDefaultsToOffWhenKeyAbsent): n/a
- Details: This validates the "default off" requirement of AC-4: callers must opt in explicitly by passing config["enable_deduplication"]=true.

#### `TEST_F(ContentSHA256DedupTest, PerceptualDedupSkippedWhenPolicyDisabled)`
- Source: `tests/content/test_content_deduplication.cpp`:533
- Brief: Verify that passing config["enable_deduplication"]=false skips the perceptual dedup check even when a DeduplicationChecker is attached.
- Parameters:
  - `<unnamed>` (ContentSHA256DedupTest): n/a
  - `<unnamed>` (PerceptualDedupSkippedWhenPolicyDisabled): n/a
- Details: This validates the AC-4 requirement: deduplication is opt-in per collection via ContentPolicy; default off.
